# White-Label & Reseller Businesses: 2,805 ideas (part 2 of 3: Freight Brokering → Sales & Soft Skills Training)

[← back to index](README.md) · 1014 entries in this file · 96 categories in total

Businesses where you sell someone else's product, service or inventory under your own brand, plus open-source software that could run the operation. Supply sources are starting points for research, so check current partner terms. The regulation column flags where a license or registration is usually needed. Startup cost: Low (1896) · Medium (801) · High (108).

**Parts:** [part 1](resell-1.md) (Activities & Tickets → Franchise-Style Micro-Businesses (coffee carts, kiosks, vending routes)) · **part 2** (Freight Brokering → Sales & Soft Skills Training) · [part 3](resell-3.md) (Skincare & Cosmetics → eSIM & Travel Data)

## Freight Brokering

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Freight brokerage for small manufacturers** | Truckload capacity matched to shipments | Carriers via DAT and Truckstop load boards | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Spread between shipper rate and carrier pay | 12-18% gross margin per load | FMCSA broker authority and $75k bond | Small manufacturers via plant managers and cold calls | Medium | Small shippers lack leverage and need a reliable rep |
| **LTL freight reseller with carrier contracts** | Discounted LTL shipments on blanket pricing | e.g. Worldwide Express, GlobalTranz, carrier agent programs | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Markup per shipment over contract rate | 15-25% markup | Broker authority or agent agreement | E-commerce sellers shipping pallets via marketplaces | Low | Aggregated volume unlocks discounts individuals never get |
| **Freight agent under host brokerage** | Loads moved under host authority | Agent-friendly brokerages with back office | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase) | Commission split of gross margin | 60-70% of margin | Host holds FMCSA authority and bond | Shippers from prior industry relationships | Low | Zero compliance overhead and instant credit lines |
| **Ocean freight NVOCC reseller** | FCL and LCL container space | Ocean carriers, consolidators, co-loaders | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Per-container spread over carrier rates | $100-400 per container | FMC OTI license and bond | Importers of furniture and machinery via trade shows | Medium | Small importers get ignored by large forwarders |
| **Air-cargo GSA for small airlines** | Cargo capacity sales on behalf of airlines | Regional airlines' cargo departments | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Commission on cargo revenue sold | 3-5% of cargo revenue | IATA CASS participation | Forwarders in one airport market via sales visits | Medium | Airlines outsource sales in markets they cannot staff |
| **Produce and reefer freight specialist** | Refrigerated truckloads for growers | Reefer carriers and owner-operators | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Margin per load | 12-16% per load | FMCSA authority, PACA awareness | Growers and packers via produce associations | Medium | Perishables pay premiums for reliability |
| **Car-shipping broker** | Auto transport on open and enclosed carriers | Car-hauler carriers via Central Dispatch | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm) | Flat fee per vehicle | $150-300 per vehicle | FMCSA broker authority and bond | Relocating families and dealers via SEO | Low | Consumers search online and compare three quotes |
| **Heavy-haul and project cargo broker** | Oversize and overweight moves | Specialized heavy-haul carriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [nextcloud/server](https://github.com/nextcloud/server) | Margin per move | 10-15% per move | FMCSA authority, permit compliance | Equipment dealers and wind-energy contractors via referrals | Medium | Complex moves command high fees and loyalty |
| **Intermodal rail drayage broker** | Rail containers with drayage legs | IMCs, railroads, dray carriers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase) | Margin per container | 8-12% per container | FMCSA authority, IMC agreements | Shippers moving 1,000-plus miles via cost pitch | Medium | Rail saves 15-30% versus truck on long hauls |
| **Digital freight-quote widget for e-commerce** | Instant pallet and parcel quotes on checkout | Rate APIs from carriers and brokers | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [directus/directus](https://github.com/directus/directus) | Per-shipment fee or margin | $5-15 per shipment | Broker authority if arranging freight | Shopify merchants selling bulky goods via app listings | Low | Bulky-goods sellers lose carts to unclear shipping |
| **Hazmat freight specialist** | Hazmat-certified carrier capacity | Hazmat-endorsed carriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Premium margin per load | 15-20% per load | FMCSA authority, hazmat documentation rules | Chemical distributors and battery shippers via associations | Medium | Few brokers understand hazmat paperwork |
| **Expedited and hotshot freight brokerage** | Same-day hotshot and sprinter capacity | Hotshot owner-operators and expedite carriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Margin per load | 15-25% per load | FMCSA authority and bond | Plants with line-down emergencies via maintenance managers | Low | Emergencies are price-insensitive |
| **Freight audit and pay service** | Carrier invoice audits and payment processing | Shippers' carrier invoices | [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Per-invoice fee or savings share | $1-3 per invoice or 30% savings | None | Mid-size shippers via CFO outreach | Low | Invoice errors run 3-5% of freight spend |
| **Truck-parking reservation marketplace** | Reserved truck parking spots | Lot owners, truck stops, industrial sites | [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Booking commission | 15-20% per booking | Local zoning for parking use | Fleet safety managers via trucking associations | Low | Parking shortage is drivers' top complaint |
| **Moving-company lead broker** | Qualified household-move leads | Licensed movers buying leads | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [mautic/mautic](https://github.com/mautic/mautic) | Per-lead fee | $20-60 per lead | FMCSA household-goods broker registration | Local movers via sales calls | Low | Movers pay for leads every busy season |
| **Flatbed freight specialist for steel and lumber** | Flatbed and step-deck capacity | Flatbed carriers and owner-operators | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Margin per load | 12-18% per load | FMCSA authority, securement rules awareness | Steel service centers and lumber yards via visits | Medium | Flatbed needs tarping know-how most brokers lack |
| **US-Mexico cross-border truckload broker** | Cross-border loads with transfer at Laredo | Mexican and US carriers, customs brokers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Margin per load | 12-18% per load | FMCSA authority, customs documentation | Maquiladora suppliers via trade associations | Medium | Nearshoring boom outpaces bilingual broker capacity |
| **Air freight consolidator China-to-US for e-commerce** | Consolidated air cargo space | Forwarders and airline block-space holders | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Per-kilo markup | $0.30-1 per kilo | IAC indirect air carrier certification | Amazon sellers via seller forums | Medium | Small shippers get spot rates far above consolidator rates |
| **Liquid bulk tanker freight broker** | Tanker trailers for food and chemicals | Tanker carriers with wash-out certifications | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Margin per load | 10-15% per load | FMCSA authority, kosher and food-grade rules | Food ingredient makers via industry shows | Medium | Tanker capacity is scarce and specialized |
| **Livestock transport broker for ranchers** | Cattle and hog hauling capacity | Livestock haulers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm) | Margin per load | 10-15% per load | FMCSA authority, animal welfare transport rules | Ranchers via sale barns and auctions | Low | Sale barns need reliable haulers every week |
| **Boat and RV transport broker** | Marine and RV hauling | Specialized boat and RV haulers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Flat fee per unit | $300-800 per unit | FMCSA broker authority | Dealers and buyers via marketplace listings | Low | Online boat sales create interstate transport demand |
| **Dedicated capacity contracts for regional shippers** | Committed weekly truck capacity | Small carriers wanting steady lanes | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Margin on contracted lanes | 8-12% per load | FMCSA authority and bond | Regional distributors via lane analysis pitches | Low | Both sides prefer predictability over spot chaos |
| **Freight claims recovery service for shippers** | Damage and loss claim filing | Shippers' claims against carriers | [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Contingency share of recovered claims | 25-35% of recovery | Carmack Amendment knowledge, no license | Furniture and electronics shippers via brokers | Low | Shippers abandon claims that are recoverable |
| **Rail carload and transload brokerage** | Boxcar and hopper capacity with transloading | Short-line railroads, transload facilities | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase) | Margin per carload | $200-600 per car | Rail contract terms, STB awareness | Lumber mills and grain elevators via visits | Medium | Short lines want sales help to win carload traffic |
| **LTL consolidation pool for small e-commerce brands** | Pooled pallet shipments to retail DCs | Consolidation warehouses, LTL carriers | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Per-pallet fee | $30-80 per pallet | Broker authority | DTC brands entering Target and Walmart via consultants | Low | Retailer chargebacks punish small brands shipping alone |
| **Carrier vetting and onboarding service for small brokers** | Carrier compliance checks and packets | FMCSA data, insurance verification APIs | [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n), [nocodb/nocodb](https://github.com/nocodb/nocodb) | Per-carrier fee or monthly | $5-15 per carrier vetted | None | New brokerages via broker Facebook groups | Low | Double-brokering fraud makes vetting essential |
| **Truck dispatch service for owner-operators** | Load finding and paperwork for truckers | Load boards and brokers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Percent of gross load revenue | 5-10% of gross | Must not act as unlicensed broker | Owner-operators via truck stops and Facebook | Low | Drivers pay to stay driving instead of negotiating |
| **White-label TMS for small shippers** | Branded shipment management portal | Open-source TMS software, carrier APIs | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Monthly SaaS plus freight margin | $200-500 monthly per shipper | Broker authority if booking freight | Shippers with 20-plus loads monthly via demos | Low | Sticky software locks in freight volume |
| **Oversize-load permit and escort coordination service** | State permits and pilot-car scheduling | State DOT permit systems, escort companies | [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Per-permit fee plus escort markup | $50-150 per permit | State permit service rules | Heavy-haul carriers via trucking associations | Low | Carriers hate multi-state permit paperwork |
| **Alcohol freight broker with licensed carriers** | Beer, wine and spirits truckloads | Alcohol-permitted carriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Margin per load | 12-18% per load | FMCSA authority, state alcohol transport permits | Craft breweries and distilleries via guilds | Medium | Compliance complexity keeps generalist brokers out |
| **Pharmaceutical cold-chain freight broker** | Temperature-validated reefer capacity | GDP-compliant reefer carriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Premium margin per load | 15-22% per load | FMCSA authority, GDP documentation | Generic drug distributors via quality managers | Medium | Validated carriers are scarce and shippers pay up |

## Game Publishing

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Indie game publishing label** | Publishing and distribution rights to indie titles | Indie developers found at jams and itch.io | [godotengine/godot](https://github.com/godotengine/godot), [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse) | Revenue share after recoupment | 30-50% of net revenue | Platform publisher agreements, ratings compliance | Solo devs via Discord servers and game jams | Medium | Devs trade revenue for marketing and platform know-how |
| **Mobile game reskin studio** | Licensed game source codes rethemed | e.g. Codester, SellMyApp source marketplaces | [godotengine/godot](https://github.com/godotengine/godot), [appwrite/appwrite](https://github.com/appwrite/appwrite) | Ad and in-app purchase revenue | $0.05-0.30 ARPDAU | App store policies, COPPA for kids titles | Casual players via ad networks | Low | Proven mechanics plus fresh themes ship in weeks |
| **Porting service to consoles** | Porting work via contracted engineers | Porting studios and freelance engine programmers | [godotengine/godot](https://github.com/godotengine/godot), [makeplane/plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane) | Fixed fee plus royalty on ported sales | 20-30% markup on porting labor | Console developer program approval | PC indie studios via publisher referrals | Medium | Studios lack console dev kits and certification experience |
| **Game localization and QA subcontracting** | LQA testers and translators | QA vendors and freelance linguists | [WeblateOrg/weblate](https://github.com/WeblateOrg/weblate), [makeplane/plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane) | Hourly markup on tester time | 25-40% on hourly rates | Data NDAs for unreleased builds | Mid-size studios via producers on LinkedIn | Low | Launch crunch makes studios outsource testing |
| **Multiplayer backend hosting for indies** | Managed game servers and matchmaking | Cloud servers, bare-metal hosts | [heroiclabs/nakama](https://github.com/heroiclabs/nakama), [coollabsio/coolify](https://github.com/coollabsio/coolify) | Monthly fee per concurrent users | 40-60% gross margin | Data-protection rules for player data | Indie multiplayer devs via engine forums | Medium | Indies cannot run backend ops alone |
| **Branded advergames for companies** | Custom HTML5 marketing games | Freelance game developers | [godotengine/godot](https://github.com/godotengine/godot), [directus/directus](https://github.com/directus/directus) | Per-project fee | 30-40% project margin | Sweepstakes rules if prizes offered | Marketing agencies via campaign pitches | Low | Agencies resell playable campaigns at premium |
| **Educational game licensing to schools** | Curriculum-aligned learning games | Edu game developers | [godotengine/godot](https://github.com/godotengine/godot), [moodle/moodle](https://github.com/moodle/moodle) | Per-school annual license | 30-50% of license | Student data privacy laws (COPPA, FERPA) | District curriculum directors via edtech conferences | Low | Districts buy proven content through trusted vendors |
| **Board-game publishing via contract manufacturers** | Printed board games from designers' prototypes | e.g. Panda, LongPack, Ludo Fact manufacturers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Product margin on retail and direct sales | 40-55% gross margin | Toy safety testing (ASTM, CE) | Hobby gamers via Kickstarter and conventions | Medium | Designers want publishing not manufacturing headaches |
| **Esports tournament organizer for brands** | Tournament production and broadcast | Production crews, casters, venues | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse) | Per-event production fee | 20-30% event margin | Prize-competition and sweepstakes rules | Brand marketers via esports agencies | Medium | Brands want gaming audiences without building teams |
| **Game key reseller (authorized)** | Legitimate game keys from distributors | e.g. Nexway, Genba, publisher distribution programs | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [saleor/saleor](https://github.com/saleor/saleor) | Margin per key | 5-15% margin | Distribution agreements, no grey-market keys | Deal hunters via price-comparison listings | Low | Authorized status beats grey markets on trust |
| **Game-server rental for Minecraft communities** | Modded Minecraft server instances | Dedicated server providers | [Paymenter/Paymenter](https://github.com/Paymenter/Paymenter), [FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling](https://github.com/FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling) | Monthly per-server fee | 40-60% gross margin | Minecraft EULA server monetization rules | Server admins via YouTube and Discord | Low | Communities pay monthly and rarely migrate |
| **Arcade and VR-pod placement operator** | Arcade and VR machines in venues | Arcade distributors, VR pod makers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Revenue split with host venues | 50-70% of coin drop | Amusement device licenses locally | Bowling alleys and malls via owner visits | High | Venues add attractions with no capital |
| **Tabletop RPG module publishing** | Licensed adventures and supplements | Freelance writers and artists | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [TryGhost/Ghost](https://github.com/TryGhost/Ghost) | Per-download and print sales | 50-70% gross margin | Third-party licenses (OGL, Creative Commons) | RPG players via DriveThruRPG and Reddit | Low | Evergreen catalog sells for years |
| **White-label game community platform** | Hosted forums, wikis and launchers | Self-hosted community tools | [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse), [BookStackApp/BookStack](https://github.com/BookStackApp/BookStack) | Monthly per-community fee | 50-70% gross margin | Content moderation and data rules | Studios launching titles via publisher partners | Low | Studios want owned communities beyond Discord |
| **Kids' game app bundles for telcos** | Curated kids' game subscriptions | Independent kids' game developers | [godotengine/godot](https://github.com/godotengine/godot), [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill) | Revenue share with telco billing | 30-40% of subscription | COPPA and kids' advertising rules | Telco value-added-services managers via partnerships | Medium | Carrier billing reaches families without cards |
| **Western-market publishing for Asian indie studios** | Steam and console publishing in English markets | Studios in Japan, Korea, China, Vietnam | [WeblateOrg/weblate](https://github.com/WeblateOrg/weblate), [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse) | Revenue share on western sales | 25-40% of net revenue | Export and platform agreements | Studios via BitSummit and Taipei Game Show | Medium | Great games with no western marketing presence |
| **Retro game re-release label licensing dormant IPs** | Emulated re-releases of classic titles | Rights holders of 80s-90s catalogs | [godotengine/godot](https://github.com/godotengine/godot), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Royalty share on re-release sales | 30-50% after royalties | IP licensing agreements | Retro fans via collector communities | Medium | Nostalgia sells with near-zero development |
| **Vinyl soundtrack releases for indie games** | Pressed vinyl and cassette soundtracks | Pressing plants, game composers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Product margin on limited pressings | 30-40% gross margin | Music licensing from composer and studio | Game fans via studio newsletters | Medium | Limited editions sell out at launch |
| **Merchandise store operator for indie game studios** | Branded apparel and collectibles | Print-on-demand and plush manufacturers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Revenue share with studio | 20-30% of merch revenue | Licensing agreement with studio | Studios with 100k-plus players via outreach | Low | Studios have fans but no merch operations |
| **User-acquisition and ad-mediation service for indie mobile games** | Ad network accounts and campaign management | e.g. AppLovin, Unity Ads, ironSource | [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Percent of ad spend plus performance bonus | 10-15% of spend | Privacy rules (ATT, GDPR) | Small mobile studios via indie forums | Low | Studios need UA expertise they cannot hire |
| **Trailer and capsule-art production outsourcing for indies** | Launch trailers and store assets | Freelance editors and illustrators | [makeplane/plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane), [nextcloud/server](https://github.com/nextcloud/server) | Per-project fee | 30-40% project margin | None | Studios approaching Steam launch via Twitter | Low | Store assets drive wishlists and conversions |
| **Console certification and ratings submission service** | Cert testing and ESRB, PEGI submissions | Testing labs and ratings consultants | [makeplane/plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Per-title fee | 30-40% margin on lab fees | Console publisher program rules | Indie studios via porting partners | Low | Certification failures delay launches by months |
| **Remote playtesting panel service for studios** | Recruited testers with recorded sessions | Panel recruitment firms, gamers | [formbricks/formbricks](https://github.com/formbricks/formbricks), [Chocobozzz/PeerTube](https://github.com/Chocobozzz/PeerTube) | Per-test fee | 40-50% margin | Participant consent and data rules | Mid-size studios via producer networks | Low | Studios need fresh eyes before launch |
| **Discord community management outsourcing for studios** | Moderators and community managers | Freelance community managers | [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse), [kimai/kimai](https://github.com/kimai/kimai) | Monthly retainer per community | 30-40% on labor | Content moderation obligations | Studios launching early access via publisher referrals | Low | Communities need 24-hour coverage studios cannot staff |
| **Game asset-pack store under own brand** | Licensed 3D models, sprites and sounds | Freelance artists and sound designers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [saleor/saleor](https://github.com/saleor/saleor) | Revenue share per sale | 30-50% of sale | Licensing terms clarity | Indie devs via engine marketplaces | Low | Evergreen catalog with zero inventory |
| **3D-printable miniature licensing to print farms** | STL files and print licenses | Miniature sculptors | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse) | Monthly license per print shop | 30-50% of license | IP licensing agreements | Local 3D print shops via maker communities | Low | Print shops need content and sculptors need distribution |
| **Puzzle game licensing to newspapers and apps** | Daily puzzle feeds and games | Puzzle constructors | [directus/directus](https://github.com/directus/directus), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-publication monthly license | 40-60% of license | Content licensing agreements | Regional newspapers via publisher associations | Low | Publishers need engagement content on budget |
| **HTML5 casual game portal with licensed titles** | Web games monetized with ads | e.g. GameDistribution, independent HTML5 devs | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [matomo-org/matomo](https://github.com/matomo-org/matomo) | Ad revenue share | 30-50% of ad revenue | Privacy and ad compliance | Casual players via SEO | Low | Portals compound traffic from long-tail titles |
| **Influencer key distribution service for launches** | Managed streamer and press key campaigns | Studios' key allocations, creator databases | [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n), [nocodb/nocodb](https://github.com/nocodb/nocodb) | Per-campaign fee | 40-50% margin | Disclosure rules for sponsored content | Studios launching on Steam via publishers | Low | Studios cannot vet thousands of key requests |
| **Kickstarter campaign management for board-game creators** | Campaign setup, fulfillment coordination | Manufacturers, fulfillment hubs | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Percent of funds raised | 8-12% of raise | Consumer protection on crowdfunding promises | First-time designers via BGG forums | Low | Creators underestimate logistics and marketing |

## Gift Cards & Loyalty

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Multi-merchant local gift card program** | Town-wide gift cards usable at local shops | Card printers, closed-loop processors | [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill), [blnkfinance/blnk](https://github.com/blnkfinance/blnk) | Breakage plus merchant fees | 3-5% merchant fee plus breakage | State gift card and escheat rules | Downtown merchant associations via chamber of commerce | Medium | Shop-local campaigns pull dollars from chains |
| **Corporate incentive gift card distributor** | Bulk brand gift cards at discount | e.g. Blackhawk, InComm, Tango Card aggregators | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Spread between discount and face value | 2-5% spread | Money-transmission exemptions for closed-loop cards | HR and sales-incentive managers via LinkedIn | Low | Companies buy millions in cards and want service |
| **Employee rewards and recognition platform** | Points redeemable for gift cards | Gift card APIs, reward catalogs | [frappe/hrms](https://github.com/frappe/hrms), [blnkfinance/blnk](https://github.com/blnkfinance/blnk) | Per-employee fee plus redemption spread | $2-4 per employee plus 3-5% | Tax reporting on rewards | HR leads at 200-2,000 employee firms via demos | Low | Recognition tools are sticky and renew yearly |
| **White-label loyalty app for coffee shops** | Digital stamp cards and rewards | Self-hosted backends, app shells | [appwrite/appwrite](https://github.com/appwrite/appwrite), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Monthly per-location fee | $30-60 per location monthly | Privacy rules for customer data | Independent cafes via roaster partnerships | Low | Cafes want Starbucks-like loyalty without the cost |

## Hosting & Domains

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Domain and hosting reseller for local businesses** | Domains and shared hosting | e.g. registrar reseller programs, VPS providers | [FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling](https://github.com/FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling), [hestiacp/hestiacp](https://github.com/hestiacp/hestiacp) | Markup per domain and plan | 30-60% on hosting | ICANN registrar terms via upstream | Local shops via chambers of commerce | Low | Bundled setup and support beats self-serve giants for non-technical owners |
| **Managed VPS reseller with control panel** | VPS instances | e.g. Hetzner, OVHcloud, DigitalOcean | [Paymenter/Paymenter](https://github.com/Paymenter/Paymenter), [hestiacp/hestiacp](https://github.com/hestiacp/hestiacp) | Monthly markup 50-200% | 50-200% over raw VPS | none | Freelance developers via forums | Low | Panel plus backups and support justify markup over raw cloud servers |
| **Game-server hosting brand** | Game server instances | e.g. dedicated server providers | [Paymenter/Paymenter](https://github.com/Paymenter/Paymenter), [heroiclabs/nakama](https://github.com/heroiclabs/nakama) | Monthly per-slot pricing | 40-70% gross | none | Gaming communities via Discord and Reddit | Medium | Community-run branding and mod installs attract loyal recurring players |
| **Niche TLD domain storefront for creatives** | Domain registrations | e.g. ICANN registrar reseller APIs | [FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling](https://github.com/FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling), [PowerDNS/pdns](https://github.com/PowerDNS/pdns) | Margin per registration | $2-8 per domain | ICANN rules via upstream registrar | Designers and artists via Instagram | Low | Curated creative names and portfolio bundles differentiate from bulk registrars |
| **Private cloud hosting for regulated SMEs** | Dedicated cloud tenants | e.g. colocation providers | [apache/cloudstack](https://github.com/apache/cloudstack), [OpenNebula/one](https://github.com/OpenNebula/one) | Monthly contract | 25-40% gross | Data residency and compliance audits | Clinics and accountants via IT consultants | High | Local data residency and compliance paperwork is hard for hyperscalers to offer |
| **Managed DNS service for agencies** | Authoritative DNS | e.g. anycast DNS hosts | [PowerDNS/pdns](https://github.com/PowerDNS/pdns), [FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling](https://github.com/FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling) | Per-zone monthly | 60-80% gross | none | Web agencies via partner programs | Low | White-label dashboard lets agencies bill DNS to clients under own brand |
| **cPanel-alternative reseller hosting** | Shared hosting accounts | e.g. dedicated servers | [froxlor/froxlor](https://github.com/froxlor/froxlor), [usmannasir/cyberpanel](https://github.com/usmannasir/cyberpanel) | Monthly per account | 50-70% gross | none | Freelancers via web-design communities | Low | Free panels avoid per-account licence fees that eat reseller margin |
| **SSL certificate reseller** | Commercial SSL certificates | e.g. CA reseller programs | [FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling](https://github.com/FOSSBilling/FOSSBilling), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Margin per certificate | 15-40% per cert | CA reseller agreement | Agencies and small ecommerce stores | Low | OV and EV certificates still need paperwork that buyers outsource |
| **App PaaS hosting for indie developers** | Docker app hosting | e.g. cloud servers | [coollabsio/coolify](https://github.com/coollabsio/coolify), [dokku/dokku](https://github.com/dokku/dokku) | Per-app monthly | 60-80% gross | none | Indie hackers via Hacker News and X | Low | Heroku-style simplicity at flat price without per-seat fees |
| **GPU server rental reseller for AI startups** | GPU instances | e.g. GPU cloud wholesalers | [Paymenter/Paymenter](https://github.com/Paymenter/Paymenter), [apache/cloudstack](https://github.com/apache/cloudstack) | Hourly markup | 15-35% over wholesale | none | AI startups via accelerator programs | High | Reserved wholesale capacity resold in small slices to teams priced out |

## Logistics & 3PL

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |

| **Discounted shipping-label reseller** | Carrier label rates | e.g. carrier reseller programs | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Per-label markup | $0.20-0.80 per label | Carrier reseller terms | Small sellers via marketplaces | Low | Pooled volume unlocks rates small sellers cannot get |
| **Cross-border e-commerce fulfillment to Canada/US** | Bonded fulfillment | e.g. customs brokers, 3PLs | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Per-parcel fee | 15-25% on cost | Customs broker involvement | Cross-border sellers via forums | Medium | Duty-paid delivery lowers abandoned carts across borders |
| **Kitting and subscription-box assembly** | Kitting labor | e.g. co-packers, 3PLs | [inventree/InvenTree](https://github.com/inventree/InvenTree), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Per-kit fee | 20-35% on labor | none | Subscription box startups | Low | Flexible short runs for brands too small for big 3PLs |
| **Returns-processing and refurbishment service** | Reverse logistics | e.g. returns centers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [zammad/zammad](https://github.com/zammad/zammad) | Per-return fee | 20-35% gross | none | E-commerce brands via ops managers | Medium | Faster restocking recovers margin on returned goods |
| **Temperature-controlled fulfillment for food brands** | Cold-chain storage | e.g. cold-storage warehouses | [openboxes/openboxes](https://github.com/openboxes/openboxes), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Per-pallet monthly markup | 15-25% on storage cost | Food handling licences | Food startups via incubators | High | Few small-volume cold options exist for emerging brands |
| **Fulfillment network broker for small sellers** | Matched 3PL capacity | e.g. 3PL marketplaces | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Referral fee or per-order | 5-15% referral | none | Small sellers via marketplace groups | Low | Matching to right-size warehouses saves sellers time |

| **Event and trade-show freight handling** | Show freight logistics | e.g. exhibition forwarders | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Per-crate margin | 15-25% gross | none | Exhibitors via organizers | Low | Exhibitors want one point of contact for shipments |

| **Humanitarian supply-chain services for NGOs** | Warehouse and distribution | e.g. NGO warehouses | [openboxes/openboxes](https://github.com/openboxes/openboxes), [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext) | Management fee | 8-15% management fee | Customs and donor compliance | NGOs via grant partners | Medium | Donor-grade reporting is hard for NGOs to build alone |

## MVNO & Mobile

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| **Faith-based or affinity-group mobile brand** | Branded MVNO plans | e.g. MVNO-in-a-box providers | [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill), [mautic/mautic](https://github.com/mautic/mautic) | Revenue share per subscriber | $2-6 per sub monthly | Telecom registration | Congregations via leaders | Medium | Group loyalty reduces churn and acquisition cost |
| **Kids' safe-phone plan reseller** | Child-safe phone plans | e.g. MVNE providers | [AdguardTeam/AdGuardHome](https://github.com/AdguardTeam/AdGuardHome), [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill) | Monthly plan margin | 20-35% on plan | Child online safety rules | Parents via schools | Medium | Filtered data and location controls reassure parents |

## Mortgage & Loan Brokering

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Mortgage broker under wholesale lenders** | Wholesale mortgage products | e.g. wholesale lenders | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Broker comp 1-2.75% (NMLS license) | 1-2.75% of loan | NMLS licence | Home buyers via agents | Medium | Access to many lenders beats single-bank rates |
| **SBA loan packaging service** | SBA 7(a) loan applications | e.g. SBA lenders | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Packaging fee plus referral (lending rules) | $2k-6k per package | Packager fee disclosure rules | Small businesses via accountants | Low | Complex paperwork is outsourced by owners who lack time |
| **Equipment-finance broker for contractors** | Equipment leases | e.g. equipment lenders | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | 2-5% broker fee (state licensing) | 2-5% of lease | State broker licensing | Contractors via dealers | Low | Dealer partnerships bring financing at point of sale |
| **Merchant cash advance ISO** | MCA funding | e.g. MCA funders | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Commission points (state disclosure laws) | 6-10 points per deal | State MCA disclosure laws | Small merchants via lead lists | Low | Fast approvals for businesses banks decline |
| **Student-loan refinance referral site** | Refinance offers | e.g. lender affiliate programs | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [mautic/mautic](https://github.com/mautic/mautic) | Per-funded-loan bounty | $200-1000 per loan | Advertising disclosure rules | Graduates via search content | Low | Educational content ranks and converts without a lending licence |
| **Auto-loan broker for credit-challenged buyers** | Subprime auto loans | e.g. indirect auto lenders | [espocrm/espocrm](https://github.com/espocrm/espocrm), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Dealer/lender referral fee (license) | $200-600 per loan | State broker or dealer rules | Buy-here dealers via networks | Low | Dealers pay for approvals on tricky credit files |

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |

## Real Estate Referrals

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |

| **Agent-referral network for relocating buyers** | Vetted agent introductions in destination cities | e.g. licensed agents and brokerages nationwide | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [mautic/mautic](https://github.com/mautic/mautic) | Referral fee share on closed deals | 25-35% of agent commission | Real estate broker license to receive fees | Relocating professionals via employer HR partners | Low | Agents pay for warm leads; relocation is a predictable trigger |

| **Branded paper bags and shopping-bag sourcing for boutiques** | Custom printed paper bags | e.g. bag factories | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Per-order markup | 30-45% markup | Import and labeling rules | Boutiques via retail associations | Low | Low minimums for small shops |

## Reselling Satellite & Rural Internet

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Starlink-style satellite kit installer and reseller** | Satellite broadband kits | e.g. authorized satellite resellers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [librenms/librenms](https://github.com/librenms/librenms) | Install fee plus residual | $100-300 per install | Authorized reseller agreement | Remote cabins and farms via ads | Medium | Mounting and cable skills are the real unmet need |
| **Satellite internet for construction sites and mining camps** | Temporary satellite service | e.g. satellite wholesalers | [zabbix/zabbix](https://github.com/zabbix/zabbix), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Monthly service markup | 15-30% markup | Telecom reseller registration | Contractors via project managers | High | Rapid deployment where terrestrial networks do not reach |
| **Rural community Wi-Fi hubs using satellite backhaul** | Shared community access | e.g. satellite wholesalers | [hotspotbilling/phpnuxbill](https://github.com/hotspotbilling/phpnuxbill), [LibreQoE/LibreQoS](https://github.com/LibreQoE/LibreQoS) | Prepaid voucher sales | 30-50% gross | Local telecom registration | Villages via NGOs | Medium | Shared vouchers make connectivity affordable per household |
| **Maritime satellite connectivity for fishing fleets** | Marine satellite plans | e.g. maritime satellite resellers | [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill), [librenms/librenms](https://github.com/librenms/librenms) | Monthly plan markup | 15-30% markup | Maritime radio rules | Fleet owners via ports | High | Safety communications and crew welfare drive demand |
| **RV and overlanding satellite subscription bundles** | Mobile satellite plans | e.g. satellite resellers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill) | Hardware margin plus residual | 10-20% hardware margin | Reseller agreement | RV owners via forums | Low | Mounts and power kits sold with service |
| **Emergency-communication kits for NGOs** | Satellite kits with batteries | e.g. satellite distributors | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [snipe/snipe-it](https://github.com/snipe/snipe-it) | Kit margin plus airtime | 15-25% gross | Export and licensing rules | NGOs via procurement | Medium | Pre-configured kits deploy in hours during disasters |
| **Fixed wireless plus satellite hybrid for ranches** | Hybrid connectivity | e.g. tower owners, satellite resellers | [LibreQoE/LibreQoS](https://github.com/LibreQoE/LibreQoS), [librenms/librenms](https://github.com/librenms/librenms) | Monthly fee | 40-55% gross | Local telecom registration | Ranchers via co-ops | Medium | Failover between links improves reliability on remote properties |
| **School connectivity programs in remote regions** | School satellite connections | e.g. satellite wholesalers | [moodle/moodle](https://github.com/moodle/moodle), [librenms/librenms](https://github.com/librenms/librenms) | Contract per school | 15-25% gross | Public procurement rules | Education ministries via tenders | High | Government programmes fund remote schools |
| **Telehealth-ready connectivity for rural clinics** | Business satellite service | e.g. satellite resellers | [zabbix/zabbix](https://github.com/zabbix/zabbix), [netbirdio/netbird](https://github.com/netbirdio/netbird) | Monthly service fee | 20-35% gross | Health data rules | Rural clinics via health networks | Medium | Reliable video quality is needed for specialist consults |
| **Pre-paid satellite data for backpackers and overlanders** | Pay-as-you-go satellite data | e.g. satellite resellers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill) | Per-GB markup | 20-40% markup | Reseller agreement | Adventure travellers via outfitters | Low | Short-term data with no contract suits trips |
| **Solar-powered rural internet kiosks** | Off-grid connectivity | e.g. solar and satellite vendors | [hotspotbilling/phpnuxbill](https://github.com/hotspotbilling/phpnuxbill), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Voucher sales plus device fees | 30-45% gross | Local telecom registration | Operators via microfinance | High | Power and connectivity packaged where grid is absent |
| **Satellite IoT connectivity for agriculture sensors** | Low-bandwidth IoT data | e.g. satellite IoT providers | [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard), [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill) | Per-device monthly | 30-45% gross | Telecom registration | Agri-tech firms | Medium | Sensors work in fields without cellular coverage |

| **Pay-as-you-go satellite internet for off-grid cabins** | Prepaid satellite plans | e.g. satellite resellers | [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Monthly plan markup | 10-20% markup | Reseller agreement | Cabin owners via real-estate agents | Low | Seasonal pause plans match occasional use |

## Sales & Soft Skills Training

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **White-label sales methodology course for agencies** | Licensed sales training content delivered under your brand | e.g. sales training publishers, SCORM libraries | [moodle/moodle](https://github.com/moodle/moodle), [frappe/lms](https://github.com/frappe/lms) | Per-learner license markup | 40-60% gross margin | Content license terms | Marketing agencies upselling training to clients | Low | Publishers want reach; agencies want new revenue lines |
| **Leadership-skills program for first-time managers** | Licensed leadership curriculum with live cohorts | e.g. leadership framework licensors, facilitators | [openedx/edx-platform](https://github.com/openedx/edx-platform), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Cohort fee per manager | 50-60% gross margin | None | HR directors at 200-plus employee firms | Low | New-manager failure is costly and universal |
| **Customer-service training for hospitality** | Guest-service e-learning modules for hotels and restaurants | e.g. hospitality training publishers | [moodle/moodle](https://github.com/moodle/moodle), [chamilo/chamilo-lms](https://github.com/chamilo/chamilo-lms) | Per-property annual subscription | 60-70% gross margin | None | Independent hotels via hospitality associations | Low | High turnover creates constant onboarding demand |
| **Public-speaking bootcamps under own brand** | Licensed presentation curriculum run by contract coaches | e.g. presentation-skills publishers, freelance coaches | [frappe/lms](https://github.com/frappe/lms), [bigbluebutton/bigbluebutton](https://github.com/bigbluebutton/bigbluebutton) | Ticketed cohort fee | 50-60% gross margin | None | Professionals via meetup groups and LinkedIn | Low | Evergreen skill with willingness to pay for live practice |
| **Negotiation skills micro-learning app** | Microlearning content packs in a branded app | e.g. microlearning content vendors | [frappe/lms](https://github.com/frappe/lms), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Per-seat monthly subscription | 70-80% gross margin | None | Procurement and sales teams via LinkedIn | Low | Bite-size format fits busy sellers and renews easily |
